# Research (Phase 1)

The research and decisions behind **Learn Chinese**, a phone-first web app for one learner in the Netherlands, at about HSK 1–2, who wants to learn the HSK vocabulary and to speak in three situations: travelling in mainland China, visiting a partner's family from south or central China, and working with Chinese colleagues and clients. Everything here was researched and checked on **2 November 2026** (2026-11-02); facts that change carry that date.

**How the research was done.**

- **Parallel research agents.** Twenty research topics, from the HSK word lists to hosting, were each given to a research agent; the agents worked in parallel and wrote down every finding with its sources.
- **An independent verifier for every finding.** A second agent that had not done the research then went through every finding and every phrase, tip and fact: it re-read the cited sources, looked for at least one further source and gave a verdict (confirmed, corrected, unverifiable or refuted). Corrections replaced the original text; the old wording is kept in the research files and the data. Of the 31 travel facts, 20 went through a second check, with fresh searches and a skeptic ([3.3](#33-travel-facts-that-change-checked-2-november-2026)).
- **Sources per item.** Every finding, phrase, tip and fact records its sources (address, publisher, access date and the quoted evidence), and every HSK word the list it comes from and the dictionary it was checked against. [SOURCES.md](SOURCES.md) lists all of them (1,437 sources on 341 sites), and the data files carry them item by item ([DATA.md](DATA.md)).
- **A dictionary check of all Chinese text.** Every HSK word, phrase, kinship term and grammar or pitfall example was compared word by word with CC-CEDICT (release 2026-05-18). An HSK word that differs needs a recorded decision, and a phrase or example that fails is not shown; the validator, which runs on every push, enforces both ([1.9](#19-pinyin-checked-against-cc-cedict), [3.1](#31-where-the-content-comes-from-and-how-it-was-checked)).
- **What the research could reach.** The research environment could fetch GitHub, npm and PyPI directly, but most websites only through web search, which returns a search engine's summary instead of the page. The web-search budget (200 searches per turn, shared by all agents) ran out during the first research pass. Some verifications therefore rely on primary sources mirrored on GitHub (official exam papers and syllabi, law texts, CCTV news transcripts, vendor documentation and source code, package files), and whatever could not be confirmed that way stays **UNVERIFIED**. Official Chinese and Dutch government sites could not be opened directly; they were read through search summaries or through copies on GitHub.

**How to read it.**

- Links are the sources: each claim links to what it rests on. Links into this repository (`../app/…`, `../data/…`, `../scripts/…`) point to the code or data that carries out a decision.
- **UNVERIFIED** marks anything that could not be confirmed. Such an item is never shown in the app as fact: content items stay in the data, out of the app, and are listed in [UNVERIFIED.md](UNVERIFIED.md); research findings are reported here and collected under [Open questions](#open-questions).
- *Decision* gives the conclusion of each part, *Today* what the app does on 2 November 2026, and *Later* what is not done yet. Numbers such as 2.3 refer to the sections below.

**Related documents:** [SOURCES.md](SOURCES.md) (every source, with licence and access date), [UNVERIFIED.md](UNVERIFIED.md) (what the app hides, and why), [AI-CONTENT.md](AI-CONTENT.md) (what AI agents wrote or assembled, for review), [DATA.md](DATA.md) (the data contract) and [SYNC.md](SYNC.md) (sign-in and sync).

**Contents:** [Decisions at a glance](#decisions-at-a-glance) · [1. HSK](#1-hsk-which-lists-which-levels-the-exam-today) · [2. Dictionary, sentences, audio, characters, frequency, pinyin](#2-dictionary-sentences-audio-characters-frequency-pinyin-tooling) · [3. The three speaking situations](#3-the-three-speaking-situations) · [4. Learning method and plan](#4-learning-method-scheduler-and-a-realistic-plan) · [5. Speaking practice technology](#5-speaking-practice-technology) · [6. Hosting, offline and the framework](#6-hosting-offline-and-the-framework) · [Open questions](#open-questions) · [What is UNVERIFIED](#what-is-unverified)

## Decisions at a glance

| Decision | Choice | Why | Section |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Word list | The 2025 HSK exam syllabus (CLEC, 11,000 entries), parsed from the official PDF; the default decks are HSK 1–3 (1,000 entries, 995 words in the app) | It is the list of the new HSK 1–6 exam, whose first regular sitting is on 13 December 2026 | [1.3](#13-the-2025-hsk-exam-syllabus), [1.10](#110-recommendation-what-to-learn-and-how-every-word-is-tagged) |
| Level tags | Three tags on every word: 2025 syllabus, GF 0025-2021 and HSK 2.0 (2012 revision); never a bare "HSK 3.0" | "HSK 3.0" names two different lists, which give the same level to only 58.5% of the words they share | [1.1](#11-two-different-documents-are-both-called-hsk-30), [1.10](#110-recommendation-what-to-learn-and-how-every-word-is-tagged) |
| Dictionary | CC-CEDICT release 2026-05-18 (CC BY-SA 4.0), pinned through an npm copy; every reading that differs gets a recorded decision | Open licence and a plain format; 98.4% of the app's words match one of its readings, and every other word has a recorded decision | [2.1](#21-dictionary-cc-cedict), [1.9](#19-pinyin-checked-against-cc-cedict) |
| Audio | Native recordings (audio-cmn) only where the file name fixes the reading, 7,383 of 12,366 words; the device's voice, labelled, for the rest; a script for Google audio is ready but has not been run | A recording named by its characters cannot prove its reading, and the device voice cannot be told one | [2.3](#23-audio) |
| Example sentences | No outside corpus; "In a phrase" cards use the app's own verified phrases | Tatoeba's per-sentence owners and licences could not be fetched, and every sentence would need a native speaker's check | [2.2](#22-example-sentences) |
| Characters | No stroke order in v1; the Characters card (off by default) asks for pinyin and meaning | The goals are vocabulary and speaking; stroke data adds offline storage and licence handling | [2.4](#24-characters-stroke-order), [4.5](#45-the-design-that-follows) |
| Frequency | wordfreq 3.1.1 ("large" Chinese list) orders the words inside each level | The clearest licence for redistribution (CC BY-SA 4.0), spoken and written sources, 98–99% of HSK words covered | [2.5](#25-word-frequency) |
| Pinyin library | The app's own module without dependencies ([app/src/lib/pinyin.js](../app/src/lib/pinyin.js)); pinyin-pro only as a second opinion during research | It handles CC-CEDICT's spellings exactly, build and app share one implementation, and no 142 KB library enters an app without a build step | [2.6](#26-pinyin-tooling) |
| Scheduler and retention | FSRS-6 through ts-fsrs 5.4.2, default weights, fuzz on; desired retention 0.90, with Lighter (0.85) and Intensive (0.95); FSRS-7 later | With default weights FSRS-6 is better calibrated than SM-2 for about 98% of the benchmark's learners; ts-fsrs 6 (FSRS-7) is days old, and switching needs a review log | [4.1](#41-the-scheduler-fsrs-not-sm-2), [4.2](#42-the-library-ts-fsrs-542-fsrs-6-now-fsrs-7-later), [4.3](#43-desired-retention) |
| Card types and defaults | Chinese → English → English → Chinese → In a phrase, Listen optional (off); changed from the earlier Listen-first default at the learner's request (see 4.5), one new type a day; at most 300 reviews a day; new words pause while reviews are overdue; decks HSK 1–3 | Ear training is the best-supported method and carries over partly to speaking; speaking is the goal | [4.5](#45-the-design-that-follows) |
| New words per day | 10 (5 if that is too much) | In the model, about 17 minutes a day after a month and 35 after a year with Listen + Speak, and every HSK 4 word met after 200 days | [4.5](#45-the-design-that-follows), [4.6](#46-a-realistic-plan) |
| Speaking technology | Self-grading, recording yourself and comparing with a native recording, and pitch lines in the tone trainer (Beta, no score); no speech recognition and no cloud scoring | None of the options can reliably judge a beginner's tones on a phone without sending the voice to a company; recordings stay on the phone | [5](#5-speaking-practice-technology), [5.4](#54-what-the-app-does-today) |
| Hosting | Vercel Hobby (free), deployed through Vercel's Git integration; the 7,359 recordings as plain files | Non-commercial personal use fits; one learner uses under 1% of the allowances; at a limit the site pauses and is never billed | [6.1](#61-vercel-hobby-terms-allowances-and-what-happens-at-a-limit), [6.2](#62-files-and-size-shipping-7359-recordings), [6.7](#67-deployment-through-github) |
| Sync | Optional sign-in and sync through Upstash Redis (Vercel Marketplace, free plan), shared with the reference project; the app works fully without it | A few JSON records per account suit a key-value store; Vercel KV and Vercel Postgres no longer exist | [6.3](#63-sync-storage-upstash-redis-not-neon) |
| Framework | No framework and no build step: plain HTML, CSS and ES modules, with a hand-written service worker for offline use | Nothing needs a build; the one library is a single self-contained file | [6.4](#64-offline-the-service-worker), [6.6](#66-no-framework-and-no-build-step) |
| AI use | No AI at runtime; AI agents did the research and the checks and arranged the practice dialogues, which are labelled and not yet reviewed | Every Chinese line shown must come from a source and pass the dictionary check; AI-assembled content is listed for review | [6.8](#68-the-claude-api-not-used-by-the-app), [3.8](#38-practice-dialogues) |

## 1. HSK: which lists, which levels, the exam today

This section answers three questions: which official HSK word list to learn, what the HSK exam looks like on 2 November 2026, and how the app's word data was built and checked. It rests on two research topics (`hsk-standard-exam` and `hsk-datasets`, both checked in a verification pass on 2026-11-02) and on the app's own data ([data/hsk/import-report.json](../data/hsk/import-report.json), [data/hsk/resolutions.json](../data/hsk/resolutions.json)). Anything the verification could not confirm is marked **UNVERIFIED** and is not presented as fact.

**The decision in one paragraph.** The app teaches the word list of the 2025 HSK exam syllabus, 《中文水平考试 HSK考试大纲》, published by the Center for Language Education and Cooperation (CLEC) in November 2025 ([official PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf)). It is the list of the new HSK 1–6 exam, whose first regular worldwide sitting is on 13 December 2026 ([CTI notice](https://www.chinesetest.cn/news/51336883), [official HSK account](https://x.com/HSKTestOfficial/status/2097633616931041329)). Every word carries three separate level tags: the 2025 syllabus, the 2021 national standard GF 0025-2021 and HSK 2.0 (2012 revision). The default study decks are 2025 levels 1–3 ([app/src/lib/settings.js](../app/src/lib/settings.js)): 1,000 list entries, which are 995 words in the app.

### 1.1 Two different documents are both called "HSK 3.0"

- From 2021 to 2025, apps, Anki decks and datasets used "HSK 3.0" for the word lists of the national standard GF 0025-2021. [ivankra/hsk30](https://github.com/ivankra/hsk30) calls its 11,092-word list "HSK 3.0 vocabulary", and [drkameleon/complete-hsk-vocabulary](https://github.com/drkameleon/complete-hsk-vocabulary) calls the same levels "new". The standard's own interpretation volume already announced a "国家级 HSK3.0 版" exam to be built on it ([GF 0025-2021, vol. 1](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2021/2021-%E5%9B%BD%E9%99%85%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%B0%B4%E5%B9%B3%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%EF%BC%88%E7%AC%AC%E4%B8%80%E5%88%86%E5%86%8C%20%20%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%8F%8F%E8%BF%B0%E3%80%81%E9%9F%B3%E8%8A%82%E3%80%81%E6%B1%89%E5%AD%97%EF%BC%89.pdf)).
- Since late 2025 the exam organiser, Chinese Testing International (CTI, 汉考国际), uses "HSK 3.0" and "3.0版HSK" for the new exam built on the 2025 syllabus ([CTI notice](https://www.chinesetest.cn/news/51336883), [CTI trial notice](https://www.chinesetest.cn/notice)). Its lists are different: 300 words at HSK 1 instead of 500 ([2025 PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf)).
- Some sites call the 2025 list "HSK 3.1" ([Hidden Dragon](https://hidden-dragon.app/blog/hsk-3-1-2026-changes-explained)); [becky82/mteh](https://github.com/becky82/mteh) uses that label, unofficially, to tell the two lists apart.
- The two documents give the same level to only 58.5% of the roughly 9,700 words they share ([harukicoder/hsk30](https://github.com/harukicoder/hsk30); recomputed in the research from [ivankra/hsk30](https://github.com/ivankra/hsk30) and the [2025 PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf)). File names mislead too: "HSK 3.0-Yufa 2025 final.csv" in [shawkynasr/HSK-official-Query-System](https://github.com/shawkynasr/HSK-official-Query-System) holds the 2021 grammar, not the 2025 one.

**Decision.** The data never stores a bare "HSK 3.0 level". Each word has three tags: `exam2025` (the 2025 exam syllabus), `std2021` (GF 0025-2021) and `hsk20` (HSK 2.0) (findings `hsk-standard-exam.naming-ambiguity`, `hsk-datasets.which-hsk30-document`). The app labels them "HSK 2025", "Standard 2021" and "HSK 2.0" ([app/src/data.js](../app/src/data.js)). **Later:** the deck group for the 2021 levels is still titled "HSK 3.0 standard (2021)" ([scripts/build-app-data.mjs](../scripts/build-app-data.mjs)); renaming it to "GF 0025-2021 standard" removes the ambiguous name from the deck list; it still appears in source titles and usage notes of the phrase and grammar data ([data/situations/travel.json](../data/situations/travel.json), [data/reference/grammar.json](../data/reference/grammar.json)).

### 1.2 The national standard GF 0025-2021

《国际中文教育中文水平等级标准》 (Chinese Proficiency Grading Standards for International Chinese Language Education), code GF 0025-2021, was issued jointly by the Ministry of Education (MOE) and the State Language Commission (国家语委) and took effect on 1 July 2021 ([MOE announcement](http://www.moe.gov.cn/jyb_xwfb/gzdt_gzdt/s5987/202103/t20210329_523304.html), [China News Service](https://www.chinanews.com.cn/cul/2021/05-21/9482134.shtml)). CLEC developed it with CTI, and the Commission's standards review committee approved it in 2020 ([vol. 1](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2021/2021-%E5%9B%BD%E9%99%85%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%B0%B4%E5%B9%B3%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%EF%BC%88%E7%AC%AC%E4%B8%80%E5%88%86%E5%86%8C%20%20%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%8F%8F%E8%BF%B0%E3%80%81%E9%9F%B3%E8%8A%82%E3%80%81%E6%B1%89%E5%AD%97%EF%BC%89.pdf)). Sources give different issue dates: MOE's news item is dated 29 March 2021 ([MOE](http://www.moe.gov.cn/jyb_xwfb/gzdt_gzdt/s5987/202103/t20210329_523304.html)), a GitHub README says 24 March 2021 ([docrealm/docs-hsk README](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/main/README.md)), and the date printed on the standard itself was not seen. This document uses the MOE date; it changes nothing for the app.

The standard grades learners in "three stages, nine levels" (三等九级): elementary (初等) levels 1–3, intermediate (中等) levels 4–6 and advanced (高等) levels 7–9. The advanced figures are not split into levels 7, 8 and 9 ([MOE Q&A](http://www.moe.gov.cn/jyb_xwfb/s271/202104/t20210402_524194.html), [vol. 1](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2021/2021-%E5%9B%BD%E9%99%85%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%B0%B4%E5%B9%B3%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%EF%BC%88%E7%AC%AC%E4%B8%80%E5%88%86%E5%86%8C%20%20%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%8F%8F%E8%BF%B0%E3%80%81%E9%9F%B3%E8%8A%82%E3%80%81%E6%B1%89%E5%AD%97%EF%BC%89.pdf)). Each level is measured on four quantities (syllables, characters, words, grammar points), plus a separate list of characters to write by hand ([MOE Q&A](http://www.moe.gov.cn/jyb_xwfb/s271/202104/t20210402_524194.html), [vol. 1](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2021/2021-%E5%9B%BD%E9%99%85%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%B0%B4%E5%B9%B3%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%EF%BC%88%E7%AC%AC%E4%B8%80%E5%88%86%E5%86%8C%20%20%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%8F%8F%E8%BF%B0%E3%80%81%E9%9F%B3%E8%8A%82%E3%80%81%E6%B1%89%E5%AD%97%EF%BC%89.pdf)).

Counts per level, written as new / cumulative. Sources: the standard's table 2 and level descriptions ([vol. 1](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2021/2021-%E5%9B%BD%E9%99%85%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%B0%B4%E5%B9%B3%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%EF%BC%88%E7%AC%AC%E4%B8%80%E5%88%86%E5%86%8C%20%20%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%8F%8F%E8%BF%B0%E3%80%81%E9%9F%B3%E8%8A%82%E3%80%81%E6%B1%89%E5%AD%97%EF%BC%89.pdf)), the [vocabulary volume](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2021/2021-%E5%9B%BD%E9%99%85%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%B0%B4%E5%B9%B3%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%EF%BC%88%E7%AC%AC%E4%BA%8C%E5%88%86%E5%86%8C%20%E8%AF%8D%E6%B1%87%EF%BC%89.pdf), the [MOE Q&A](http://www.moe.gov.cn/jyb_xwfb/s271/202104/t20210402_524194.html) for the totals, and [CTI's query-system data](https://github.com/shawkynasr/HSK-official-Query-System) for the per-level syllables and grammar points. All figures were recounted in the verification (finding `hsk-standard-exam.standard-2021-counts`).

| Level | Syllables | Characters (reading) | Words | Grammar points | Handwriting characters |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | 269 / 269 | 300 / 300 | 500 / 500 | 48 / 48 | 100 / 100 |
| 2 | 199 / 468 | 300 / 600 | 772 / 1,272 | 81 / 129 | 100 / 200 |
| 3 | 140 / 608 | 300 / 900 | 973 / 2,245 | 81 / 210 | 100 / 300 |
| 4 | 116 / 724 | 300 / 1,200 | 1,000 / 3,245 | 76 / 286 | 100 / 400 |
| 5 | 98 / 822 | 300 / 1,500 | 1,071 / 4,316 | 71 / 357 | 150 / 550 |
| 6 | 86 / 908 | 300 / 1,800 | 1,140 / 5,456 | 67 / 424 | 150 / 700 |
| 7–9 | 202 / 1,110 | 1,200 / 3,000 | 5,636 / 11,092 | 148 / 572 | 500 / 1,200 |

Notes on the table:

- **Handwriting.** The standard states 100, 200 and 300 characters of its elementary handwriting list for levels 1–3, 100, 250 and 400 of its intermediate list for levels 4–6, and stage totals of 300, 700 and 1,200 ([vol. 1](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2021/2021-%E5%9B%BD%E9%99%85%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%B0%B4%E5%B9%B3%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%EF%BC%88%E7%AC%AC%E4%B8%80%E5%88%86%E5%86%8C%20%20%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%8F%8F%E8%BF%B0%E3%80%81%E9%9F%B3%E8%8A%82%E3%80%81%E6%B1%89%E5%AD%97%EF%BC%89.pdf)). The cumulative 400, 550 and 700 for levels 4–6 are derived by adding the 300 elementary characters.
- **The handwriting list contradicts its own description.** The usage note says the 1,200 characters are all 900 elementary reading characters plus 300 intermediate ones; the printed list has 888 elementary and 312 intermediate characters, and 咖, 啡, 啤, 喂, 嘴, 搬, 箱, 蕉, 衫, 裙, 裤 and 赢 are missing ([vol. 1](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2021/2021-%E5%9B%BD%E9%99%85%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%B0%B4%E5%B9%B3%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%EF%BC%88%E7%AC%AC%E4%B8%80%E5%88%86%E5%86%8C%20%20%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%8F%8F%E8%BF%B0%E3%80%81%E9%9F%B3%E8%8A%82%E3%80%81%E6%B1%89%E5%AD%97%EF%BC%89.pdf), [elkmovie/hsk30](https://github.com/elkmovie/hsk30), [CTI's data](https://github.com/shawkynasr/HSK-official-Query-System)). The printed list is taken as correct, because the book's list pages and four independent transcriptions agree on it (finding `hsk-standard-exam.standard-2021-handwriting-composition`). The app does not use the 2021 handwriting list.
- **Flexibility.** Each level may swap or drop about 5% of its content for local needs ([vol. 1](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2021/2021-%E5%9B%BD%E9%99%85%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%B0%B4%E5%B9%B3%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%EF%BC%88%E7%AC%AC%E4%B8%80%E5%88%86%E5%86%8C%20%20%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%8F%8F%E8%BF%B0%E3%80%81%E9%9F%B3%E8%8A%82%E3%80%81%E6%B1%89%E5%AD%97%EF%BC%89.pdf)).
- **Pinyin notation** in the 2021 word list ([vol. 2, usage notes](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2021/2021-%E5%9B%BD%E9%99%85%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%B0%B4%E5%B9%B3%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%EF%BC%88%E7%AC%AC%E4%BA%8C%E5%88%86%E5%86%8C%20%E8%AF%8D%E6%B1%87%EF%BC%89.pdf)): 一 and 不 are written with their spoken tone change (一样 yíyàng, 不必 búbì); separable words are split with // (见面 jiàn//miàn); an obligatory neutral tone has no mark (包子 bāozi); a dot before a toned syllable means "usually neutral, sometimes stressed" (道理 dào·lǐ); erhua is written as base + r (玩儿 wánr); "|" joins alternative forms (爸爸|爸); brackets mark affix examples (第（第二）), optional parts (好（不）容易) and sense notes. Its pinyin is based on the 2010 grading list and checked against 《现代汉语词典》 (7th edition) and 《现代汉语规范词典》 (3rd edition) (same source).

**Decision.** GF 0025-2021 is a secondary tag (`std2021`, [scripts/import-hsk.mjs](../scripts/import-hsk.mjs)), not the list the app teaches. It is still the national standard and the basis of most "HSK 3.0" material published from 2021 to 2025, but the new exam is built on the 2025 syllabus (findings `hsk-standard-exam.data-availability`, `hsk-standard-exam.syllabus-2025-vs-standard-2021`).

### 1.3 The 2025 HSK exam syllabus

《中文水平考试 HSK考试大纲》 (Syllabus for the Chinese Proficiency Test) is published by CLEC (中外语言交流合作中心). Its cover reads "2025-11 发布 2026-07 实施": published November 2025, in force July 2026 ([official PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf)). The 406-page document contains a task syllabus, a topic syllabus, the vocabulary (levels 1–6 and one band 7–9), characters (to recognise per level, and to write) and grammar (same PDF). Press reports date its presentation to the World Chinese Language Conference on 15 November 2025 ([FLTRP](https://www.fltrp.com/c/2025-11-20/540070.shtml)), and the official HSK account announced the syllabus and sample papers as released on 18 December 2025 ([X post](https://x.com/HSKTestOfficial/status/2001605164625514917)); both dates are **UNVERIFIED** (the verification could not reopen these pages).

"July 2026" on the cover is only the nominal start date. No regular sitting of the new exam took place in July 2026. Wikipedia and several blogs repeat the cover date ([Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hanyu_Shuiping_Kaoshi)), but CTI's notice fixes the first regular sitting on 13 December 2026 ([CTI](https://www.chinesetest.cn/news/51336883)). This document follows the official notice (finding `hsk-standard-exam.launch-date-resolution`).

Counts per level, new / cumulative, parsed from the [official PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf) and confirmed by two independent extractions ([harukicoder/hsk30](https://github.com/harukicoder/hsk30), [krmanik/HSK-3.0](https://github.com/krmanik/HSK-3.0); finding `hsk-standard-exam.syllabus-2025-counts`):

| Level | Words (list entries) | Characters to recognise | Characters to write |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | 300 / 300 | 246 / 246 | 100 / 100 (one list for levels 1 and 2) |
| 2 | 200 / 500 | 125 / 371 | – / 100 |
| 3 | 500 / 1,000 | 284 / 655 | 150 / 250 |
| 4 | 1,000 / 2,000 | 441 / 1,096 | 150 / 400 |
| 5 | 1,600 / 3,600 | 431 / 1,527 | 150 / 550 |
| 6 | 1,800 / 5,400 | 413 / 1,940 | 150 / 700 |
| 7–9 | 5,600 / 11,000 | 1,148 / 3,088 | 500 / 1,200 |

- There is no syllable list. The grammar syllabus is a table of categories per level without numbered grammar points, so there is no official grammar count ([PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf)). A community transcription splits the tables into 70/78/96/95/70/50/134 rows for levels 1–6 and 7–9 ([krmanik/HSK-3.0](https://github.com/krmanik/HSK-3.0)); that is a row count, not a number of grammar points (**UNVERIFIED** as a grammar count; finding `hsk-standard-exam.syllabus-2025-grammar-count`).
- The recognition list has 3,088 distinct characters, not 3,000. harukicoder reports that secondary sources often quote 3,079 (the research did not see such a source itself); the official per-level lists add up to 3,088 ([harukicoder/hsk30](https://github.com/harukicoder/hsk30), [PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf)), so 3,088 is used.

How the 11,000 entries are written ([PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf); same finding):

- They are 10,896 distinct written forms. 99 forms appear more than once: 60 with different readings (还 hái at level 1, huán at level 3) and 39 with the same reading for a different sense or level (本1 běn at level 1, 本2 běn at level 5).
- 102 entries add a sense at a higher level, shown in brackets: 半 "1（4）" enters at level 1 as a numeral and adds its adverb use at level 4. Three carry two extra levels: 好 "1（2）（4）", 头 "2（3）（5）" and 像 "3（4）（7-9）".
- 495 entries have no part of speech (phrases such as 不客气), and 82 carry a sense number.

**How it differs from the 2021 standard.** Cumulative words per level ([HSK 2.0 syllabus introduction](https://download.chinesetest.cn/newhsk-site/Syllabus/H1_DG.pdf), [GF 0025-2021 vol. 1](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2021/2021-%E5%9B%BD%E9%99%85%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%B0%B4%E5%B9%B3%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%EF%BC%88%E7%AC%AC%E4%B8%80%E5%88%86%E5%86%8C%20%20%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%8F%8F%E8%BF%B0%E3%80%81%E9%9F%B3%E8%8A%82%E3%80%81%E6%B1%89%E5%AD%97%EF%BC%89.pdf), [2025 PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf)):

| Level | HSK 2.0 | GF 0025-2021 | 2025 syllabus |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | 150 | 500 | 300 |
| 2 | 300 | 1,272 | 500 |
| 3 | 600 | 2,245 | 1,000 |
| 4 | 1,200 | 3,245 | 2,000 |
| 5 | 2,500 | 4,316 | 3,600 |
| 6 | 5,000 | 5,456 | 5,400 |
| 7–9 | – | 11,092 | 11,000 |

The 2025 list is a re-levelled revision of the 2021 list, not a subset with the same levels. About 89% of its forms (9,707 of 10,896) occur somewhere in the 2021 list, and about 89% of the 2021 forms occur in the 2025 list, but only 58.5% of the shared words keep their level ([ivankra/hsk30](https://github.com/ivankra/hsk30), [2025 PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf), [harukicoder/hsk30](https://github.com/harukicoder/hsk30); finding `hsk-standard-exam.syllabus-2025-vs-standard-2021`). At level 1, 266 of the 300 new words were 2021 level-1 words, and everyday words moved down: 超市, 出租车, 饺子, 便宜, 漂亮, 公司, 问题 and 可以 from 2021 level 2, 苹果 from level 3, 女士 and 些 from level 4. The other way round, only 266 of the 506 written forms of 2021 level 1 stay at level 1, and 62 are not in the 2025 list at all, mostly transparent compounds such as 北京, 吃饭, 唱歌 and 车票. The 2025 list also has 玩 and 一下 where 2021 level 1 has 玩儿 and 一下儿 (same sources; finding `hsk-datasets.trap-erhua`).

Characters: 2,945 of the 3,088 recognition characters are among the 2021 standard's 3,000; 143 are new (for example 嗯, 狐, 狸, 蝴, 蝶, 螃, 蟹) and 55 of the 2021 characters were dropped, mostly surname and place-name characters (刘, 吴, 赵, 沪, 浙, 粤). Both handwriting lists have 1,200 characters but share only 606, and in 2025 each level's characters to write are a subset of that level's characters to recognise ([2025 PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf), [ivankra/hsk30](https://github.com/ivankra/hsk30); finding `hsk-standard-exam.syllabus-2025-characters-vs-2021`).

**Two versions of the PDF circulate**, with the same 11,000 entries, levels and character lists. Version A (the mirror the app uses, file modified 15 January 2026) writes 没有, 有点儿, 有时, 差点儿, 要不 and 凡是. Version B (the December 2025 file, transcribed by krmanik) writes 没（有）, 有（一）点儿, 有时（候）, 差（一）点儿, 要不（然） and 凡（是）. The order within each level also differs, so only 1,379 of the 11,000 entry numbers carry the same word in both ([2025 PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf), [krmanik/HSK-3.0](https://github.com/krmanik/HSK-3.0), [becky82/mteh](https://github.com/becky82/mteh)). That A is the later revision is inferred from file metadata (medium confidence), and which version the official site serves today was not checked (finding `hsk-standard-exam.syllabus-2025-versions`).

**Decision.** The 2025 syllabus is the primary list (`exam2025`), in version A, pinned by its SHA-256 (ec74ce04…) in [data/sources.lock.json](../data/sources.lock.json). Entry numbers are kept as provenance but never used as keys, because they differ between versions (findings `hsk-standard-exam.syllabus-2025-identity`, `hsk-standard-exam.syllabus-2025-versions`).

### 1.4 HSK 2.0, the exam being replaced

HSK 2.0 (新汉语水平考试, six levels, introduced in 2009–2010) has cumulative vocabularies of 150, 300, 600, 1,200, 2,500 and "5,000 or more" words for levels 1 to 6 ([HSK syllabus introduction, 2015](https://download.chinesetest.cn/newhsk-site/Syllabus/H1_DG.pdf)). The same document maps the levels to CEFR A1–C2; that is CTI's own claim and was not evaluated. The word lists exist in two editions, the 2009–10 original and a revision labelled 2012: 316 of 4,798 shared words changed level, 195 words are only in the 2010 edition and 197 only in the 2012 one ([glxxyz/hskhsk.com](https://github.com/glxxyz/hskhsk.com), [gigacool/hanyu-shuiping-kaoshi](https://github.com/gigacool/hanyu-shuiping-kaoshi)). The official 2009 HSK 1 syllabus confirms the 2009–10 placements, for example 火车站 at level 1 ([2009 HSK 1 syllabus](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%202/%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2/2009-%E6%96%B0%E6%B1%89%E8%AF%AD%E6%B0%B4%E5%B9%B3%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%20HSK%28%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%29.pdf)). Which edition the exam actually used from 2013 to 2026, and the dating of the revision to late 2012, are **UNVERIFIED**: only secondary sources say so ([harukicoder/hsk30](https://github.com/harukicoder/hsk30); finding `hsk-datasets.counts-hsk20-and-editions`).

For a learner coming from HSK 2.0 the start overlaps well: 146 of the 150 HSK 2.0 (2012) level-1 words are new HSK 1 words; 前面 and 后面 move to level 2, 小姐 to level 5, and 北京 is not an entry ([harukicoder/hsk30](https://github.com/harukicoder/hsk30), [2025 PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf); finding `hsk-standard-exam.hsk20-syllabus`).

**Decision.** The `hsk20` tag comes from the 2012 revision in [glxxyz/hskhsk.com](https://github.com/glxxyz/hskhsk.com), the only independent open copy of that edition (harukicoder's 2012 table is taken from it), and only the word and its level are used (recommendations of `hsk-standard-exam` and `hsk-datasets`). Earlier on 2 November 2026 the import used [clem109/hsk-vocabulary](https://github.com/clem109/hsk-vocabulary), which carries the 2009–10 edition; it was replaced the same day to follow the research.

### 1.5 The exam on 2 November 2026

The HSK 1–6 exam is in transition. The regular HSK 1–6 sittings of 2026 have used the HSK 2.0 papers, and the new exam has run only as two global trials, on 31 January and 20 September 2026 ([CTI trial notice](https://www.chinesetest.cn/notice), [second trial notice](https://admin.chinesetest.cn/gonewcontent.do?id=51236758), [harukicoder/hsk30](https://github.com/harukicoder/hsk30)). The first regular worldwide sitting of the new HSK 1–6, built on the 2025 syllabus, is on 13 December 2026 ([CTI notice](https://www.chinesetest.cn/news/51336883), [official HSK account](https://x.com/HSKTestOfficial/status/2097633616931041329), [神州学人网](http://www.chisa.edu.cn/general/202609/t20260909_2111519112.html)). The next HSK 1–6 exam the learner can book is therefore the new one. Whether HSK 2.0 is still offered anywhere after that date is **UNVERIFIED**: no official statement was found, and third-party guides disagree ([StudyCLI](https://studycli.org/hsk/the-new-hsk/), [Hilingo](https://www.hilingo.cn/news/95.html); finding `hsk-standard-exam.hsk20-end-date`).

| Date | Event | Status |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 Jul 2021 | GF 0025-2021 takes effect ([MOE](http://www.moe.gov.cn/jyb_xwfb/gzdt_gzdt/s5987/202103/t20210329_523304.html)) | verified |
| 26 Nov 2022 | First global HSK 7–9 sitting ([CTI notice](https://old.chinesetest.cn/gonewcontent.do?id=46783143), [press report](https://news.qq.com/rain/a/20221107A060ME00)) | **UNVERIFIED** |
| Nov 2025 | 2025 syllabus published; cover "2025-11 发布 2026-07 实施" ([PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf)) | verified |
| 19 Dec 2025 – 15 Jan 2026 | Registration for the first trial: paper-based until 7 Jan, internet-based until 15 Jan ([China News Service](https://www.chinanews.com.cn/edu/2025/12-19/10536380.shtml)) | **UNVERIFIED** |
| 31 Jan 2026 | First global trial of the new HSK 1–6 ([CTI](https://www.chinesetest.cn/notice), [神州学人网](http://www.chisa.edu.cn/general/202602/t20260202_2111443246.html)) | verified |
| 8–9 Sep 2026 | CTI announces the launch on 13 Dec 2026 ([CTI](https://www.chinesetest.cn/news/51336883)) | launch verified; announcement date **UNVERIFIED** |
| 20 Sep 2026 | Second global trial ([CTI](https://admin.chinesetest.cn/gonewcontent.do?id=51236758)) | verified |
| 7 Nov 2026 | Sitting that third-party guides call the last HSK 2.0 date ([StudyCLI](https://studycli.org/hsk/the-new-hsk/), [HSK Exam Guide](https://hskexam.org/guides/hsk-test-dates-2026/)) | **UNVERIFIED** |
| 16 Nov 2026, 24:00 Beijing time | Registration deadline for 13 Dec, paper-based ([CTI](https://www.chinesetest.cn/news/51336883)) | **UNVERIFIED** |
| 22 Nov 2026 | HSK 7–9 sitting ([CTI 2026 calendar](https://admin.chinesetest.cn/gonewcontent.do?id=50278989)) | **UNVERIFIED** |
| 3 Dec 2026, 24:00 Beijing time | Registration deadline for 13 Dec, internet-based ([CTI](https://www.chinesetest.cn/news/51336883)) | **UNVERIFIED** |
| 13 Dec 2026 | First regular worldwide sitting of the new HSK 1–6 ([CTI](https://www.chinesetest.cn/news/51336883)) | verified |

The **UNVERIFIED** dates come from the research phase's reading of official notices, press reports and third-party guides; the verification could not reopen them (no web searches left, official sites blocked). Anyone booking should confirm them on [CTI's test-dates page](https://www.chinesetest.cn/test-dates); if they are right, the paper-based deadline of 16 November is two weeks away.

The new HSK 1–6 papers, from CTI's 《新版HSK（1-6级）考试结构与样题示例》 and CLEC's sample papers, mirrored in [docrealm/docs-hsk](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk) (finding `hsk-standard-exam.new-exam-structure`):

| Level | Listening + reading + writing items | Total items | Time, about | Speaking paper |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | 20 + 20 + 0 | 40 | 40 min | none |
| 2 | 25 + 25 + 10 | 60 | 60 min | none |
| 3 | 30 + 30 + 10 | 70 | 83 min | 15 items (repeat 8, describe pictures 5, answer 2), about 15 min including 6 min preparation |
| 4 | 32 + 32 + 6 | 70 | 85 min | 5 items, about 20 min |
| 5 | 35 + 35 + 2 | 72 | 110 min | 5 items, about 23 min |
| 6 | 40 + 40 + 2 | 82 | 125 min | 5 items, about 23 min |

Pinyin is printed with the questions on the new HSK 1 and 2 sample papers, but not on HSK 3 (except as prompts in its character-writing part) or HSK 4, and writing characters is tested from HSK 2 ([docrealm/docs-hsk](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk)). One HSK 3 speaking question in the sample paper is 请介绍一下你的一位家人。 ("Please introduce one of your family members."), which fits the family goal of this app. Pass marks and score scales of the new levels were not found.

- **HSK 7–9** is a separate exam that is already running, with one paper whose result is reported as level 7, 8, 9 or below 7. The 2026 sample paper has 91 items in about 200 minutes (listening 40, reading 40, writing 2, translation 4, speaking 5), down from 98 items in about 210 minutes in the 2022–2025 papers ([docrealm/docs-hsk](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk), [CTI HSK 7–9 page](https://www.chinesetest.cn/HSK/7-9)). Its first global sitting on 26 November 2022, internet-based delivery only, and the 2026 sittings on 9 May and 22 November are **UNVERIFIED** ([CTI](https://old.chinesetest.cn/gonewcontent.do?id=46783143), [press report](https://news.qq.com/rain/a/20221107A060ME00), [CTI 2026 calendar](https://admin.chinesetest.cn/gonewcontent.do?id=50278989); finding `hsk-standard-exam.hsk79-status`).
- **Speaking (HSKK).** In the new exam, speaking belongs to the level: HSK 3, 4, 5 and 6 each have their own speaking paper, HSK 1 and 2 have none, and HSK 7–9 includes speaking ([docrealm/docs-hsk](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk)). That a speaking paper must be registered together with the written test of the same level is **UNVERIFIED** ([CTI](https://www.chinesetest.cn/notice)). Whether the old, separate HSKK (初级/中级/高级) continues after the switch is **UNVERIFIED**: no official text says ([CTI test dates](https://www.chinesetest.cn/test-dates), [HSK Exam Guide](https://hskexam.org/guides/hsk-test-dates-2026/); finding `hsk-standard-exam.hskk-standalone-future`).
- **Certificates.** An HSK score report counts as proof of level for admission for two years from the test date ("成绩自考试日起两年内有效", [HSK syllabus introduction](https://download.chinesetest.cn/newhsk-site/Syllabus/H1_DG.pdf)); the result itself stays on record. No official rule for converting HSK 2.0 levels into new levels was found. CTI's 2022 statement that certificates already obtained remain valid ([Jiamusi University republication](https://iec.jmsu.edu.cn/info/1043/1436.htm)) and the two-year validity of the trial reports ([CTI](https://www.chinesetest.cn/notice)) are **UNVERIFIED** (finding `hsk-standard-exam.validity-transition`).

**Decision.** The app presents the 2025 syllabus as the exam list "from 13 December 2026" and never presents HSK 2.0 as the current exam ([app/src/views/decks.js](../app/src/views/decks.js), [app/src/views/settings.js](../app/src/views/settings.js); findings `hsk-standard-exam.exam-status-2026-11-02`, `hsk-standard-exam.launch-date-resolution`). It stores no other exam dates; they are recorded only here. **Later:** recheck the **UNVERIFIED** dates on CTI's site before relying on them.

### 1.6 Datasets evaluated

Each dataset was cloned and counted in the research on 2026-11-02 (findings `hsk-datasets.candidate-*`, `hsk-datasets.official-2025-pdf-row-level-check`). "Last commit" is the newest commit seen that day.

| Dataset | Licence | Format and fields | Counts | Last commit | Known errors | Role in the app |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [Official 2025 syllabus PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf), mirrored in [docrealm/docs-hsk](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk) | none stated (official publication; the mirror has no licence) | 406-page PDF with a text layer; rows: number, level (extra levels in brackets), word (homograph digit), pinyin as printed, part of speech | 11,000 entries, 300/200/500/1,000/1,600/1,800/5,600; 10,896 distinct forms | file modified 2026-01-15; mirror commit bdff233 of 2026-10-18 | apparent typos 任性 rénxìng and 脚踏实地 jiǎotāshídì, disputed 温差 wēnchà (section 1.9); no apostrophe in 疼爱 and 感恩 (section 1.8) | **Primary:** `exam2025` levels and official pinyin |
| [krmanik/HSK-3.0](https://github.com/krmanik/HSK-3.0) | CC BY-SA 4.0 (License.md) | TSV, TXT and JSON; 2025 dump with the same five columns; 2021 lists as characters only; Anki decks | 2025 dump: 11,000 rows; 2021: 500/771/973/1,000/1,071/1,140/5,636 lines | 2026-10-24 | 2025 dump: 76 rows differ from the PDF (66 ASCII apostrophes, 6 bracketed spellings, 线上 xiàshàng, 怎么办 zěnmebàn, 汉服 hànfú, a cut-off part of speech for 多), and its numbers are not the PDF's. 2021 list: OCR errors that elkmovie fixed in 2021 (鑾 for 攀, 宙 for 审, 人手 for 入手). Anki deck gives 了 the traditional form 瞭 | Cross-check only |
| [harukicoder/hsk30](https://github.com/harukicoder/hsk30) ([PyPI](https://pypi.org/project/hsk30/)) | MIT (tables); CC BY 4.0 (reader corpus) | TSV, one row per distinct word: word, level, also, pinyin, pos; also tables for 2021 and both HSK 2.0 editions | 10,896 words: 300/197/491/990/1,579/1,777/5,562 | 2026-10-05 | pinyin of all 64 multi-word entries cut at the first space; second readings dropped (还 huán, 得 dé and děi, 行 háng) | Cross-check only |
| [ivankra/hsk30](https://github.com/ivankra/hsk30) | MIT (data from an official publication) | CSV: ID, Simplified, Traditional, Pinyin (cleaned), POS, Level, WebNo, WebPinyin, OCR, Variants, CEDICT (the expanded file has Example instead of Variants) | 11,092 rows = 500/772/973/1,000/1,071/1,140/5,636; expanded file 11,165 rows | 2023-11-08 (only commit) | cleaned Pinyin drops "·" (138 rows) and "∥"; grammar file has 573 points (official 572); 7 traditional forms not in CC-CEDICT | **`std2021`** levels |
| [shawkynasr/HSK-official-Query-System](https://github.com/shawkynasr/HSK-official-Query-System) | MIT | CSV copy of CTI's query-system data for the 2021 standard: words, characters, syllables, handwriting, grammar | 11,092 words; 1,110 syllables; 1,200 handwriting characters; 572 grammar points | 2026-10-09 | older 词汇.csv gives 会 the pinyin huìlǜ (fixed in 词汇 2022.csv); stray ‘ in 接二连三 | Reference: ivankra matches it row for row |
| [elkmovie/hsk30](https://github.com/elkmovie/hsk30) | MIT (Pleco) | plain text, characters only (OCR of the MOE PDF) | 11,092 rows, exact | 2023-11-08 | earlier OCR errors were fixed in 2021; one row is numbered 1856 instead of 1836 | Not used (no pinyin) |
| [drkameleon/complete-hsk-vocabulary](https://github.com/drkameleon/complete-hsk-vocabulary) | MIT label, but the meanings are CC-CEDICT text (CC BY-SA) | JSON, 11,470 records with old/new/newest level tags and every CC-CEDICT reading | old 150/150/299/601/1,298/2,500; new 506/750/953/972/1,059/1,123/5,606; newest misses 839 syllabus words | 2026-03-23 | does not say which reading a level belongs to (879 records with several readings); one level per word; affix examples given levels; pinyin bugs ("u:", "r5") | Not used |
| [glxxyz/hskhsk.com](https://github.com/glxxyz/hskhsk.com) | MIT | tab-separated, one file per level (BOM, CRLF): simplified, traditional, numbered pinyin, pinyin, English | 2012 revision: 150/151/300/600/1,300/2,500 rows | 2021-05-04 | 221 of 4,996 traditional forms wrong; 27 cells hold two readings; typos 哪 "nǎa" and 墨水儿 "mòshuǐer"; 一/不 tone changes printed in 36–38 rows; definitions partly CC-CEDICT text | **`hsk20`** levels (word and level only) |
| [clem109/hsk-vocabulary](https://github.com/clem109/hsk-vocabulary) | MIT | JSON per level: id, hanzi, pinyin, translations | 2009–10 edition: 150/150/299/601/1,300/2,500 | 2019-10-29 | "v" for ü in 8 words; 使劲儿 "shǐ jìn ér"; 打篮球 "dá"; 扇子 "shān zi"; 兴高采烈 "xīng" | Used until 2 Nov 2026, then replaced |
| [gigacool/hanyu-shuiping-kaoshi](https://github.com/gigacool/hanyu-shuiping-kaoshi) | unclear: the licence file says MIT, the data header says CC BY-NC | CSV and JSON | as clem109, which copied it | 2020-11-14 | as clem109 | Not used |
| [plaktos/hsk_csv](https://github.com/plaktos/hsk_csv) | none | CSV generated from gigacool, one English gloss per word | – | 2018-07-26 | inherits gigacool's | Not used |
| [tonghuikang/HSK-3.0-words-list](https://github.com/tonghuikang/HSK-3.0-words-list) | krmanik's licence file | copy of krmanik's older repository; no 2025 data | – | 2024-02-14 | its README says the meanings "may contain errors" | Not used |

The research first proposed krmanik's dump as the 2025 source, because it was the only complete open file with one row per entry. It switched to the official PDF once the verifier could read the PDF itself: the PDF is the exam's own document, while the dump differs from it in 76 rows and in its numbering (recommendation of `hsk-datasets`). Parsing the PDF also avoids the share-alike terms of krmanik's lists, although the app's word data is CC BY-SA 4.0 anyway because of CC-CEDICT ([CC-CEDICT](https://www.mdbg.net/chinese/dictionary?page=cc-cedict); finding `hsk-datasets.licensing`).

### 1.7 What the app uses

**The 2025 list is read from the official PDF itself.** [scripts/lib/syllabus2025.mjs](../scripts/lib/syllabus2025.mjs) takes the PDF's text (`pdftotext -layout`) and turns each vocabulary row into number, level, extra levels, word, sense digit, pinyin as printed and part of speech. Nothing is guessed: a row that does not parse, or a number between 1 and 11,000 that is missing or repeated, stops the import ([scripts/import-hsk.mjs](../scripts/import-hsk.mjs)). The PDF comes from the [docrealm mirror](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk) at commit bdff233 and is checked against its SHA-256 ([data/sources.lock.json](../data/sources.lock.json)). The mirror is a third-party upload, because chinesetest.cn could not be reached from the research sandbox; its authenticity rests on the CLEC cover, a 406-page layout that matches [harukicoder's description](https://github.com/harukicoder/hsk30) of the official file, and agreement with [krmanik's independent transcription](https://github.com/krmanik/HSK-3.0) on 99.3% of rows (finding `hsk-datasets.official-2025-pdf-row-level-check`). The parsed rows are committed as [data/sources/hsk2025-syllabus.json](../data/sources/hsk2025-syllabus.json), so the data can be reviewed without the PDF. Result: all 11,000 rows, 300/200/500/1,000/1,600/1,800/5,600 per level, 102 rows with extra levels, 82 sense-numbered rows and 495 rows without a part of speech, as the research counted.

A cross-check was run once for this section (2026-11-02; it is not part of the build). The parsed rows agree with [harukicoder's independent extraction](https://github.com/harukicoder/hsk30) on the level of all 10,896 distinct words and on the pinyin of all 10,832 entries whose pinyin has no space (harukicoder cuts the other 64). They agree with [krmanik's dump](https://github.com/krmanik/HSK-3.0) on 10,990 of the 11,000 rows once apostrophes and spacing are made uniform (krmanik keeps a space inside the part of speech of 高龄); the other 10 are exactly the known krmanik differences (the six bracketed spellings, 线上, 怎么办, 汉服 and the part of speech of 多), and 1,379 numbers carry the same word in both, as the research found.

**The 2021 levels** come from [ivankra/hsk30](https://github.com/ivankra/hsk30) `hsk30-expanded.csv` at commit 4ff9e39: 11,165 rows, with each variant and optional form on its own row, read through its Pinyin column (一/不 already in citation tones; "∥" and "·" removed; [scripts/fetch-sources.mjs](../scripts/fetch-sources.mjs), [scripts/import-hsk.mjs](../scripts/import-hsk.mjs)).

**The HSK 2.0 levels** come from [glxxyz/hskhsk.com](https://github.com/glxxyz/hskhsk.com) "HSK Official With Definitions 2012" L1–L6 at commit 8a6f229, word and level only (no traditional forms, no English definitions). Cells with two readings ("cháng, zhǎng") become two rows, so 5,001 rows become 5,028, and one typo is corrected in the import, 哪 "nǎa" to nǎ ([scripts/import-hsk.mjs](../scripts/import-hsk.mjs), `SOURCE_CORRECTIONS`).

**Merging.** One word is one set of simplified characters with one citation reading. A 2021 or HSK 2.0 row joins the 2025 word with the same characters and syllables when the tones are equal or differ only in neutral against full tone (HSK 2.0 学生 xuésheng joins 2025 学生 xuéshēng); otherwise it becomes a word of its own ([scripts/import-hsk.mjs](../scripts/import-hsk.mjs)). The result today ([data/hsk/import-report.json](../data/hsk/import-report.json)):

| Words in the app | Count |
| --- | --- |
| From the 2025 list, by first level 1 / 2 / 3 / 4 / 5 / 6 / 7–9 | 300 / 198 / 497 / 997 / 1,591 / 1,792 / 5,583 = 10,958 |
| Only in GF 0025-2021 and/or HSK 2.0 (file words-other.json) | 1,408 |
| Total | 12,366 |

The 2025 numbers are slightly below the entry counts because 42 words appear twice with the same reading and are stored once, keeping both rows: 点1 (level 1) and 点2 (level 2) are one word at level 1, and 花1 and 花2 (both level 2) are one word. The study decks follow the 2025 levels. The 2021 levels (509/755/958/986/1,063/1,128/5,622 words) and the HSK 2.0 levels (150/152/303/603/1,310/2,502) are offered as other views of the same words ([app/data/manifest.json](../app/data/manifest.json)).

### 1.8 Data traps and how the pipeline handles each

The traps come from the dataset audit (findings `hsk-datasets.trap-*`); the handling was checked in the code and data on 2026-11-02 ([scripts/import-hsk.mjs](../scripts/import-hsk.mjs), [data/hsk/](../data/hsk/)).

| Trap | What the sources do | What the pipeline does |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Same characters, same reading, two entries** (homographs) | 39 of the 99 repeated forms in the 2025 list have the same reading: 38 are numbered (本1 běn level 1, 本2 běn level 5), 横 héng appears at levels 6 and 7–9 without digits, and 为, 调 and 露 mix digits with different readings ([2025 PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf)). | Keys are characters plus reading, never digits alone. Same-reading rows stay inside one word with all their numbers, levels, senses and parts of speech (`exam2025[]`); 42 words carry two rows, e.g. 本 běn as a measure word at level 1 and as an adverb or pronoun at level 5. |
| **One character, several readings** (polyphones) | 60 repeated forms have different readings, each with its own level: 行 xíng level 3, háng level 5; 得 de level 2, dé and děi level 3 ([2025 PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf)). harukicoder keeps only the lowest row, drkameleon gives every reading one level, glxxyz puts two readings in one cell ([harukicoder](https://github.com/harukicoder/hsk30), [drkameleon](https://github.com/drkameleon/complete-hsk-vocabulary), [glxxyz](https://github.com/glxxyz/hskhsk.com)). Taking the first CC-CEDICT entry is wrong for 53 of 1,000 readings in 2025 levels 1–3 (都 gives the surname Dū first, 还 Huán, 说 shuì) ([CC-CEDICT](https://registry.npmjs.org/@leonsilicon%2fcc-cedict)). | Different readings are different words. Readings are compared with all CC-CEDICT readings, English meanings come only from entries with the same reading (or one that differs only in a neutral tone), and a word recording (named by its characters) is used only when CC-CEDICT knows exactly one reading and it equals ours ([scripts/import-hsk.mjs](../scripts/import-hsk.mjs)). |
| **Neutral tone** | The official lists leave obligatory neutral tones unmarked (东西 dōngxi). The 2021 list marks 138 rows with "·" (学生 xué·shēng, "usually neutral, sometimes stressed"); the 2025 list prints them with full tones (学生 xuéshēng, 关系 guānxì) where CC-CEDICT has a neutral tone, and the reverse also occurs (网上 wǎngshang, CC-CEDICT wǎng shàng) ([vol. 2](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2021/2021-%E5%9B%BD%E9%99%85%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%B0%B4%E5%B9%B3%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%EF%BC%88%E7%AC%AC%E4%BA%8C%E5%88%86%E5%86%8C%20%E8%AF%8D%E6%B1%87%EF%BC%89.pdf), [CTI's data](https://github.com/shawkynasr/HSK-official-Query-System), [2025 PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf)). Neither side is uniformly right; the official list is what the exam uses. | The official reading is kept: 141 words differ from CC-CEDICT only in neutral against full tone, each with a rule-based decision in [data/hsk/resolutions.json](../data/hsk/resolutions.json). **Later:** the 2021 "·" flags are lost because the import reads ivankra's cleaned Pinyin column, not WebPinyin as the research advised, and the CC-CEDICT reading is stored but not shown in the app. |
| **Erhua (儿)** | Official form is base + r (一点儿 yìdiǎnr); CC-CEDICT writes a separate syllable "r5"; clem109 and gigacool write "ér" (使劲儿 "shǐ jìn ér"); glxxyz has 墨水儿 "mòshuǐer" ([vol. 2](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2021/2021-%E5%9B%BD%E9%99%85%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%B0%B4%E5%B9%B3%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%EF%BC%88%E7%AC%AC%E4%BA%8C%E5%88%86%E5%86%8C%20%E8%AF%8D%E6%B1%87%EF%BC%89.pdf), [clem109](https://github.com/clem109/hsk-vocabulary), [gigacool](https://github.com/gigacool/hanyu-shuiping-kaoshi), [glxxyz](https://github.com/glxxyz/hskhsk.com)). | A separate "r" joins the syllable before it. For words ending in 儿, a final "ér" or "er" is also tried as r, and CC-CEDICT decides between erhua and a real ér (女儿 nǚ'ér); 墨水儿 becomes mòshuǐr. Words with and without 儿 stay separate (玩 wán, 2025 level 1; 玩儿 wánr, 2021 level 1 only). |
| **Variants, optional parts, affix examples** | The 2021 list joins variants with a vertical line (爸爸\|爸), which the datasets write with three different characters (\|, ｜, ∣), and uses brackets for affix examples (第（第二）), optional parts (有（一）点儿) and sense notes ([ivankra](https://github.com/ivankra/hsk30), [elkmovie](https://github.com/elkmovie/hsk30), [CTI's data](https://github.com/shawkynasr/HSK-official-Query-System)). The 2025 PDF (version A) has none of these, only homograph digits ([2025 PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf)). | The import reads ivankra's expanded file, which puts each variant and optional form on its own row (爸爸 and 爸; 有点儿 and 有一点儿), and keeps affix examples (第二, 科学家, 朋友们 …) as "example only" rows without a level; 16 such words are in no deck. Version A's full words are used; version B's bracketed spellings are not stored. |
| **一/不 printed with the tone change** | Both official lists write the spoken tone of 一 and 不: the 2025 list has 90 entries with yí or yì (一下 yíxià) and 57 with bú (不客气 bú kèqi), while 15 entries keep yī (统一 tǒngyī) ([2025 PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf), [vol. 2](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2021/2021-%E5%9B%BD%E9%99%85%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%B0%B4%E5%B9%B3%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%EF%BC%88%E7%AC%AC%E4%BA%8C%E5%88%86%E5%86%8C%20%E8%AF%8D%E6%B1%87%EF%BC%89.pdf)). | Readings are stored in citation tones (一 yī, 不 bù). yí/yì become yī and bú becomes bù only on syllables that belong to 一 or 不; the printed string stays in `pinyin_official`, and the app shows the spoken form under the word ("Spoken: yíxià"). 145 words of the 2025 list differ from their printed reading for this reason only. Since 2 November the same conversion runs on 2021 and HSK 2.0 rows before matching, so HSK 2.0 一下 yíxià joins the 2025 word. |
| **Traditional characters** | The only traditional character found in a simplified field of any dataset is 鑾, an OCR error for 攀 in krmanik's 2021 list; glxxyz's traditional column is wrong for 221 of 4,996 entries ([krmanik](https://github.com/krmanik/HSK-3.0), [elkmovie](https://github.com/elkmovie/hsk30), [glxxyz](https://github.com/glxxyz/hskhsk.com)). | The app is simplified-only and imports no traditional column. The validator fails on any HSK word containing a character that occurs only in CC-CEDICT's traditional headwords ([scripts/validate.mjs](../scripts/validate.mjs)). |
| **Missing apostrophes** | The 2025 PDF writes ’ in 104 entries (女儿 nǚ’ér), but its text has 疼爱 "téngài" (no. 9383) and 感恩 "gǎnēn" (no. 6692) without it ([2025 PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf), parsed rows in [data/sources/hsk2025-syllabus.json](../data/sources/hsk2025-syllabus.json)). "gǎnēn" even splits into two valid syllables, gǎ + nēn. | When the strict reading does not line up with the characters or matches CC-CEDICT worse, a lenient split is tried and the apostrophe restored: téng'ài, gǎn'ēn; the printed string is kept. **Later:** use one apostrophe style; today 104 words have ’ and 9 have ' (these two and 7 from ivankra). |
| **Extra levels in brackets** | 102 entries add a sense at a higher level: 半 "1（4）", 好 "1（2）（4）" ([2025 PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf)). | The word's level is the first number; the extra levels are stored with the row (`extra_levels`). **Later:** the app does not show them yet, and its part-of-speech chips print the bracketed parts as they are (一下: 数量 · （副）). |
| **Lists disagree on a reading** | 下载 is xiàzài in the 2025 and 2021 lists but xiàzǎi in HSK 2.0 (2012); 嗯 is ǹg in the 2025 list but ēn in HSK 2.0 ([2025 PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf), [glxxyz](https://github.com/glxxyz/hskhsk.com)). | Different readings are different words, so the app has 下载 xiàzài (2025 level 5) and 下载 xiàzǎi (HSK 2.0 level 5, not in the 2025 list), and 嗯 ǹg and ēn. **Later:** link such pairs on the word page. |
| **Typos in the source lists** | 2025: 任性, 温差, 脚踏实地 (section 1.9). glxxyz: 哪 "nǎa". clem109: 扇子 "shān zi", 兴高采烈 "xīng" ([glxxyz](https://github.com/glxxyz/hskhsk.com), [clem109](https://github.com/clem109/hsk-vocabulary)). | 2025 typos: reviewed decisions (section 1.9). 哪: corrected in the import. clem109 is no longer used. |

### 1.9 Pinyin checked against CC-CEDICT

**Method.** Every word's reading is compared with all readings that [CC-CEDICT](https://www.mdbg.net/chinese/dictionary?page=cc-cedict) (release 2026-05-18, [npm copy](https://registry.npmjs.org/@leonsilicon%2fcc-cedict)) gives for the same characters ([scripts/lib/cedict.mjs](../scripts/lib/cedict.mjs)). Every word that differs gets an explicit decision with a note in [data/hsk/resolutions.json](../data/hsk/resolutions.json), and the validator fails while any decision is pending ([scripts/validate.mjs](../scripts/validate.mjs)). Results today ([data/hsk/import-report.json](../data/hsk/import-report.json)):

| Result | Words | Decision |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Equal to one of CC-CEDICT's readings | 12,172 (98.4%) | none needed; includes the 3 corrected readings below |
| Differs only in neutral against full tone (学生 xuéshēng, CC-CEDICT xué sheng) | 141 (1.1%) | rule: keep the official reading |
| Not a CC-CEDICT headword, but every character's reading is in CC-CEDICT (phrases such as 车上 chē shang, 不太 bù tài, 能不能 néng bu néng, 打篮球 dǎ lánqiú, 致力于 zhìlìyú) | 45 (0.4%) | rule: keep the list's reading |
| Real difference, reviewed with sources, official reading kept | 8 (0.06%) | table below |
| Total | 12,366 | 199 decisions: 141 + 45 rule-based, 13 reviewed |

For comparison, the research found that the 2025 list matches CC-CEDICT exactly in 97.6% of rows, or 98.9% once the 一/不 tone changes are undone, and that neutral-tone conventions alone separate the official lists from CC-CEDICT in about 1% of entries ([krmanik](https://github.com/krmanik/HSK-3.0), [CC-CEDICT](https://registry.npmjs.org/@leonsilicon%2fcc-cedict); findings `hsk-datasets.cedict-pinyin-comparison`, `hsk-datasets.trap-neutral-tone`).

**The 13 reviewed readings.** The evidence for each (official rows, CC-CEDICT entries, dictionary quotes) is in [data/hsk/resolutions.json](../data/hsk/resolutions.json). "现汉" below is the 《现代汉语词典》 text in a community transcription, [CNMan/XDHYCD7th](https://github.com/CNMan/XDHYCD7th/blob/1e38ce15528b2fc3fdee0b2934b0951fcac51cb9/XDHYCD7th.txt), whose entries from c to z still carry the 5th-edition wording.

| Word | Official list prints | CC-CEDICT | App teaches | Why |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 任性 | 2025 [rénxìng](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf) (no. 8815) | [rèn xìng](https://www.mdbg.net/chinese/dictionary?page=cc-cedict) | rènxìng | apparent typo: the list reads 任 as rèn in its 23 other 任 words; 现汉 [rènxìng](https://github.com/CNMan/XDHYCD7th/blob/1e38ce15528b2fc3fdee0b2934b0951fcac51cb9/XDHYCD7th.txt); HSK 2.0 has [rènxìng](https://github.com/glxxyz/hskhsk.com) too |
| 温差 | 2025 [wēnchà](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf) (no. 9667) | [wēn chā](https://www.mdbg.net/chinese/dictionary?page=cc-cedict) | wēnchā | apparent typo: 差 "difference" is chā in the list's 时差, 误差, 落差, 偏差, 反差; 现汉 [wēnchā](https://github.com/CNMan/XDHYCD7th/blob/1e38ce15528b2fc3fdee0b2934b0951fcac51cb9/XDHYCD7th.txt); but 汉典 has wēnchà (see below) |
| 脚踏实地 | 2025 [jiǎotāshídì](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf) (no. 7454) | [jiǎo tà shí dì](https://www.mdbg.net/chinese/dictionary?page=cc-cedict) | jiǎotàshídì | apparent typo borrowing the tā of 踏实 tāshi: both lists read 踏 "step on" as tà (踏, 践踏, 踏上); 现汉 [jiǎotàshídì](https://github.com/CNMan/XDHYCD7th/blob/1e38ce15528b2fc3fdee0b2934b0951fcac51cb9/XDHYCD7th.txt) |
| 一晃 | yíhuàng in [2025](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf) (no. 10222) and [2021](https://github.com/ivankra/hsk30) | [yī huǎng](https://www.mdbg.net/chinese/dictionary?page=cc-cedict) | yīhuàng | both official lists agree; 现汉 has [yīhuàng](https://github.com/CNMan/XDHYCD7th/blob/1e38ce15528b2fc3fdee0b2934b0951fcac51cb9/XDHYCD7th.txt) for "time passes in a flash" |
| 下载 | xiàzài in [2025](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf) (no. 3230) and [2021](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2021/2021-%E5%9B%BD%E9%99%85%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%B0%B4%E5%B9%B3%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%EF%BC%88%E7%AC%AC%E4%BA%8C%E5%88%86%E5%86%8C%20%E8%AF%8D%E6%B1%87%EF%BC%89.pdf) | [xià zǎi, also pr. xiàzài](https://www.mdbg.net/chinese/dictionary?page=cc-cedict) | xiàzài | both official lists agree and CC-CEDICT accepts it |
| 泄露 | xièlòu in [2025](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf) (no. 9931) and [2021](https://github.com/ivankra/hsk30) | [xiè lù, also pr. xièlòu](https://www.mdbg.net/chinese/dictionary?page=cc-cedict) | xièlòu | 现汉 [xièlòu](https://github.com/CNMan/XDHYCD7th/blob/1e38ce15528b2fc3fdee0b2934b0951fcac51cb9/XDHYCD7th.txt) |
| 藤蔓 | 2025 [téngwàn](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf) (no. 9384) | [téng màn, also pr. téngwàn](https://www.mdbg.net/chinese/dictionary?page=cc-cedict) | téngwàn | 现汉 [téngwàn](https://github.com/CNMan/XDHYCD7th/blob/1e38ce15528b2fc3fdee0b2934b0951fcac51cb9/XDHYCD7th.txt) |
| 标识 | 2025 [biāoshí](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf) (no. 5638) | [biāo zhì](https://www.mdbg.net/chinese/dictionary?page=cc-cedict) (as a variant of 标志) | biāoshí | a word separate from 标志 biāozhì in the list; 现汉 has a separate entry [biāoshí](https://github.com/CNMan/XDHYCD7th/blob/1e38ce15528b2fc3fdee0b2934b0951fcac51cb9/XDHYCD7th.txt) "to mark; a mark" |
| 嗯 | 2025 [ǹg](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf) (no. 1534) | [ēn, èn, en](https://www.mdbg.net/chinese/dictionary?page=cc-cedict) | ǹg | 现汉 has a fourth-tone [ǹg](https://github.com/CNMan/XDHYCD7th/blob/1e38ce15528b2fc3fdee0b2934b0951fcac51cb9/XDHYCD7th.txt) for answering "yes" (the transcription shows its tone letter as a placeholder, next to spelled-out ńg and ňg) |
| 出血 | 2021 only, [chū∥xiě](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2021/2021-%E5%9B%BD%E9%99%85%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%B0%B4%E5%B9%B3%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%EF%BC%88%E7%AC%AC%E4%BA%8C%E5%88%86%E5%86%8C%20%E8%AF%8D%E6%B1%87%EF%BC%89.pdf) | [chū xuè](https://www.mdbg.net/chinese/dictionary?page=cc-cedict) | chūxiě | the 2021 list chooses the reading of 血 word by word ([输血 shūxuè, but 献血 xiànxiě](https://github.com/ivankra/hsk30)), so xiě looks deliberate |
| 献血 | 2021 only, [xiànxiě](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2021/2021-%E5%9B%BD%E9%99%85%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%B0%B4%E5%B9%B3%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%EF%BC%88%E7%AC%AC%E4%BA%8C%E5%88%86%E5%86%8C%20%E8%AF%8D%E6%B1%87%EF%BC%89.pdf) | [xiàn xuè](https://www.mdbg.net/chinese/dictionary?page=cc-cedict) | xiànxiě | printed 2021 list; as for 出血 |
| 谁 | shéi/shuí in [2025](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf) (no. 181) and [2021](https://github.com/ivankra/hsk30) | [shéi, also pr. shuí](https://www.mdbg.net/chinese/dictionary?page=cc-cedict) | shéi, "Also: shuí" | 现汉 [shéi 又 shuí](https://github.com/CNMan/XDHYCD7th/blob/1e38ce15528b2fc3fdee0b2934b0951fcac51cb9/XDHYCD7th.txt): one word with an alternative reading |
| 熟 | shú/shóu in [2025](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf) (no. 1688) and [2021](https://github.com/ivankra/hsk30) | [shú, also pr. shóu](https://www.mdbg.net/chinese/dictionary?page=cc-cedict) | shú, "Also: shóu" | 现汉 lists [shóu](https://github.com/CNMan/XDHYCD7th/blob/1e38ce15528b2fc3fdee0b2934b0951fcac51cb9/XDHYCD7th.txt) as colloquial for shú |

**The three apparent typos in the 2025 list.** The PDF itself prints rénxìng, wēnchà and jiǎotāshídì; the research rendered the pages, so these are not extraction errors, and 任性 is even sorted among the rén- words, so the error sits in the source spreadsheet ([2025 PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf); finding `hsk-datasets.suspected-errors-official-2025`). The research advised against silently correcting the exam's own list and asked for the words to be flagged for review. The reviewed decisions go one step further and teach the dictionary reading, because the list contradicts itself in each case and CC-CEDICT and 现汉 agree with each other. The printed reading stays in `pinyin_official`, so the decision can be reversed if a native speaker or a printed dictionary says otherwise.

The sources disagree on 温差. CC-CEDICT and 现汉 give wēnchā ([CC-CEDICT](https://www.mdbg.net/chinese/dictionary?page=cc-cedict), [现汉](https://github.com/CNMan/XDHYCD7th/blob/1e38ce15528b2fc3fdee0b2934b0951fcac51cb9/XDHYCD7th.txt)), but the 汉典 (zdic) data gives wēn chà ([mozillazg/phrase-pinyin-data](https://github.com/mozillazg/phrase-pinyin-data)), which is why the research called it disputed rather than an error. The app uses wēnchā: two dictionaries agree on it, it matches how the list itself reads 差 in every comparable noun, and 现汉 is one of the references the 2021 standard names for its pinyin ([vol. 2](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2021/2021-%E5%9B%BD%E9%99%85%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E6%B0%B4%E5%B9%B3%E7%AD%89%E7%BA%A7%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%EF%BC%88%E7%AC%AC%E4%BA%8C%E5%88%86%E5%86%8C%20%E8%AF%8D%E6%B1%87%EF%BC%89.pdf)).

**Readings that still deserve a check against a printed 现汉 (7th edition) and the 审音表.** For five of the kept readings, 现汉 was consulted only in the community transcription ([CNMan/XDHYCD7th](https://github.com/CNMan/XDHYCD7th/blob/1e38ce15528b2fc3fdee0b2934b0951fcac51cb9/XDHYCD7th.txt)), and the 审音表 not at all:

- 一晃 yīhuàng: the transcription (5th-edition wording) has yīhuàng for "time passes in a flash" and yīhuǎng for "flash past"; CC-CEDICT has only yī huǎng.
- 标识 biāoshí: the transcription's b- section carries 7th-edition wording and has biāoshí as its own entry, beside 标识 as a variant of 标志 biāozhì; CC-CEDICT has only biāo zhì.
- 下载 xiàzài: the transcription (5th-edition wording) has xiàzǎi; the printed 2021 list and the 2025 list have xiàzài, and the 2021 standard says its pinyin was checked against the 7th edition, whose entry could not be seen.
- 出血 chūxiě: the transcription has chūxiě only as a dialect word, "to pay out money or things for someone", while medical compounds read xuè (大出血 dàchūxuè), so learners will also meet chūxuè.
- 献血 xiànxiě: the transcription has no entry, so only the printed 2021 list supports xiě; CC-CEDICT has xiàn xuè.

**Two decisions were dropped.** Two reviewed decisions had been made for errors in clem109's HSK 2.0 lists, 扇子 "shān zi" (correct: shànzi) and 兴高采烈 "xīng gāo cǎi liè" (correct: xìnggāo-cǎiliè) ([clem109](https://github.com/clem109/hsk-vocabulary); finding `hsk-datasets.candidate-clem109-gigacool`). Since the HSK 2.0 source changed to glxxyz, no list produces these readings, so the import dropped both decisions; 199 remain.

### 1.10 Recommendation: what to learn and how every word is tagged

**What to learn.** Learn the 2025 list level by level, starting with levels 1–3: 1,000 entries (995 words in the app), 655 characters to recognise and 250 to write. It is the list of the exam that starts on 13 December 2026, it is smaller at the start than the 2021 standard (300 words instead of 500 at level 1, 1,000 instead of 2,245 by level 3), and it moved everyday words such as 超市, 出租车, 饺子, 便宜 and 苹果 into HSK 1 ([2025 PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf), [CTI notice](https://www.chinesetest.cn/news/51336883); recommendation of `hsk-standard-exam`). These are the app's default decks ([app/src/lib/settings.js](../app/src/lib/settings.js)).

- If you learned with HSK 2.0 or 2021-based material, many of these words will be familiar: 288 of the 995 level 1–3 words carry an HSK 2.0 level 1–2 tag, and 794 a 2021 level 1–2 tag ([data/hsk](../data/hsk/)). The "Sort: know / learn" button on a deck marks known words quickly, so study time goes to new ones ([app/src/views/deck.js](../app/src/views/deck.js)).
- Leave levels 7–9 for later; they are a separate exam that adds 5,600 entries ([CTI HSK 7–9 page](https://www.chinesetest.cn/HSK/7-9), [2025 PDF](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk/blob/bdff23362be0ffb4020505b7333a4443bcaa9ec1/HSK%203/HSK%203-2025/%E6%96%B0%E7%89%88HSK%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.pdf)).
- From level 3 on, practise reading without pinyin, because the new sample papers print pinyin on HSK 1 and 2 but not on HSK 3 and 4 ([docrealm/docs-hsk](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk)); the app's characters card, which is off until switched on in Settings, hides pinyin by default ([app/src/lib/settings.js](../app/src/lib/settings.js)).
- Practise speaking with the HSK 3 tasks: repeat a sentence, describe a picture, answer a question such as 请介绍一下你的一位家人。 ([docrealm/docs-hsk](https://github.com/docrealm/docs-hsk)).
- To sit the exam: the new HSK 1–6 starts on 13 December 2026; the registration deadlines of 16 November (paper) and 3 December (internet-based) are **UNVERIFIED**, so check [CTI's test dates](https://www.chinesetest.cn/test-dates). HSK 3 and above come with a speaking paper.

**How every word is tagged** ([docs/DATA.md](DATA.md), [scripts/import-hsk.mjs](../scripts/import-hsk.mjs)). Example: 好 hǎo.

| Field | Values | Source | 好 hǎo |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `levels.exam2025` | "1" to "6", "7-9", or null | 2025 PDF | "1" |
| `exam2025[]` | every official row: number, level, extra levels, sense, part of speech | 2025 PDF | no. 71, level 1, extra levels 2 and 4, 形、（副）、（动） |
| `levels.std2021` | "1" to "6", "7-9", or null | ivankra/hsk30 | "1" (rows L1-0138 and L2-0214) |
| `levels.hsk20` | 1 to 6, or null | glxxyz, 2012 revision | 1 |
| `pinyin` | citation tones, after reviewed decisions | 2025 PDF, else the older lists | hǎo |
| `pinyin_official` | as printed in the 2025 list | 2025 PDF | hǎo |
| `pinyin_alt` | accepted alternative reading | 2025 PDF | none (谁: shuí) |
| `cedict` | match verdict and CC-CEDICT readings | CC-CEDICT 2026-05-18 | ok |
| `resolution` | decision when the reading differs | resolutions.json | null |
| `source`, `source_url`, `verified_against` | provenance | pinned inputs | 2025 PDF, CC-CEDICT 2026-05-18 |

In the app, each word shows its three levels as chips ("HSK 2025 1", "Standard 2021 1", "HSK 2.0 1"), its citation pinyin, the spoken form when a tone change applies, alternative readings, and a "Pinyin check" line ([app/src/views/word.js](../app/src/views/word.js), [app/src/views/parts.js](../app/src/views/parts.js)).

**Left for later:**

1. Show the extra levels of the 102 bracketed entries (好: adverb at level 2, verb at level 4) and tidy the part-of-speech chips.
2. Tell the learner when the official list prints another reading (任性, 温差, 脚踏实地: the word page now says "Matches CC-CEDICT", [app/src/views/word.js](../app/src/views/word.js)), and show the CC-CEDICT reading for the 141 neutral-tone words.
3. Find English meanings for the 53 words that have none: the 8 kept readings and the 45 phrases that are not CC-CEDICT headwords.
4. Keep the 2021 "·" and separable "∥" flags by reading ivankra's WebPinyin column, as the research advised.
5. Make the harukicoder and krmanik cross-checks part of the build, failing on any new difference, as the research advised; today they were run once.
6. Check 一晃, 标识, 下载, 出血 and 献血 against a printed 现汉 (7th edition) and the 审音表, and the three corrected words with a native speaker.
7. Confirm which PDF version the official site serves, and store version B's six bracketed spellings as aliases.
8. Recheck the **UNVERIFIED** exam facts on CTI's site: deadlines, HSK 2.0 after 13 December, the standalone HSKK, certificates, HSK 7–9 dates.
9. Link words that two lists read differently (下载 xiàzài and xiàzǎi; 嗯 ǹg and ēn), and use one apostrophe style.
10. Rename the "HSK 3.0 standard (2021)" deck group.
11. Import the 2025 character lists (3,088 to recognise, 1,200 to write); a writing deck should start with the 100-character HSK 1–2 list, since the new HSK 2 already tests writing.

## 2. Dictionary, sentences, audio, characters, frequency, pinyin tooling

This section covers the reference data and tools behind every word card: the dictionary that checks pinyin and supplies meanings, example sentences, audio, stroke order, word frequency and the code that handles pinyin. Each part ends with the decision for the app, what the app does today and what is left for later.

All research was done on 2026-11-02. Several official sites (mdbg.net, tatoeba.org, Microsoft's and Apple's pages) could not be reached from the research environment, so some facts rest on mirrors or on the projects' own source code; this is said where it matters. Word and recording counts come from the app data built on 2026-11-02 ([app/data/](../app/data/)); they move slightly whenever the word lists are re-imported.

Where a finding below says "HSK 3.0", it means the lists of the 2021 standard GF 0025-2021 as the cited dataset has them (section 1.1): 11,092 rows in ivankra/hsk30, 10,969 entries in complete-hsk-vocabulary, so the counts differ with the dataset. The app itself teaches the 2025 syllabus (section 1).

| Topic | Decision for v1 | Details |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Dictionary | CC-CEDICT, release 2026-05-18, taken from an npm copy | 2.1 |
| Example sentences | No outside sentence corpus; "In a phrase" cards use the app's own verified phrases | 2.2 |
| Audio | Native recordings only where the file name fixes the reading; the device's voice, labelled, for the rest; a script for pre-generated Google audio is ready | 2.3 |
| Characters | No stroke-order animation in v1 | 2.4 |
| Word frequency | wordfreq 3.1.1 orders the words inside each level | 2.5 |
| Pinyin | The app's own module without dependencies; pinyin-pro only as a second opinion during research | 2.6 |
