- **Licences** (not legal advice, and not researched by a lawyer): whether the official HSK word lists may be redistributed in the app, whether short example sentences from the copyrighted GF 0025-2021 edition and the HSK papers may be quoted, whether a rank derived from wordfreq counts as adapted material (and the EU database right), and how to ship hanzi-writer-data's `radStrokes` field (LGPL) ([1.6](#16-datasets-evaluated), [2.4](#24-characters-stroke-order), [2.5](#25-word-frequency), [3.9](#39-pitfalls-and-grammar)).
- **HSK documents**: which HSK 2.0 edition the exam used from 2013 to 2026, the issue date printed on GF 0025-2021 ([1.2](#12-the-national-standard-gf-0025-2021)), the official release date of the 2025 syllabus, where the figure of 3,079 characters comes from, the missing official grammar count, errata reported in the dataset repositories' issue trackers, and the five kept readings 一晃, 标识, 下载, 出血 and 献血 against a printed 现代汉语词典 (7th edition) and the 审音表 ([1.3](#13-the-2025-hsk-exam-syllabus), [1.4](#14-hsk-20-the-exam-being-replaced), [1.6](#16-datasets-evaluated), [1.9](#19-pinyin-checked-against-cc-cedict)).
- **Sentences and pinyin tools**: Tatoeba sentence owners and licences, fetched from a machine that can reach tatoeba.org (and whether mdbg.net and tatoeba.org can be reached from GitHub Actions or a Vercel build); the date and format of manythings.org's file; the licence of the Chinese Grammar Wiki; pinyin-pro's actual output on the app's data; pinyin-tone 3.0.0 on CC-CEDICT's spellings; and Hanzi Writer under the app's security policy ([2.2](#22-example-sentences), [2.4](#24-characters-stroke-order), [2.6](#26-pinyin-tooling)).
- **Phrases no source showed**: "for here or to go?", "can you type it on my phone?", a picture menu, what staff say for QR-code ordering, "can you change a 100?", allergy cards, which exit or which carriage, "is this train going to …?", announcements such as 开始检票, window and aisle seat letters, and what to do after losing a passport; WeChat lines (sharing a contact card, 我加你了, 拉你进群), the text of a friend request and the expected reply speed, 免贵姓X and 叫我X就行, replies to 请多关照, 尊敬的X总 as an email opener, meeting-app words (腾讯会议, 会议号), and whether a bare 辛苦了, 嗯 or 好 is fine towards a superior; at home, 就当自己家 and 欢迎欢迎, refusing and insisting over red envelopes, and replies to questions about marriage; and an attested learner sentence for the separable-verb error \*见面你 (for 跟你见面) ([3.2](#32-travel), [3.4](#34-family), [3.5](#35-work), [3.9](#39-pitfalls-and-grammar)).
- **Customs and register no source settled**: whether 早生贵子 now sounds intrusive; Winter Solstice, Double Ninth and 本命年 customs, and sweet or savoury 粽子; the clean-plate campaign at family meals; how strictly gift taboos are kept today; English names and surname + 小姐 in offices; surname + 老板 and 领导 as forms of address, and 老X for a superior; and how often 研究研究 means a definite no ([3.4](#34-family), [3.5](#35-work), [3.6](#36-the-etiquette-that-matters-most)).
- **Regional speech**: 您 in the north and the south; southern sound mergers (zh and z, n and l, -n and -ng) and fewer neutral tones; 礼拜 and 咱们 in the south; 转 or 拐 for "turn"; and the comparisons with Dutch letters that rest on Wiktionary only. All of it is anecdotal or single-source so far ([3.7](#37-regional-notes-for-a-family-from-south-and-central-china), [3.9](#39-pitfalls-and-grammar)).

### Product and data decisions still open

- **One pinyin convention** for A-not-A forms (是不是 shì bu shì, but 可不可以 kě bù kěyǐ), for runs of three third tones (我很好 wó hén hǎo, but 我可以 wǒ kéyǐ) and for light syllables that only the 现代汉语词典 marks (舅母 jiù·mu); showing the CC-CEDICT reading next to the official one for the 141 neutral-tone words; keeping the 2021 "·" marks; and one apostrophe style ([1.8](#18-data-traps-and-how-the-pipeline-handles-each), [1.10](#110-recommendation-what-to-learn-and-how-every-word-is-tagged)).
- **When to move to FSRS-7**: once ts-fsrs 6 has settled (its default weights changed on 2026-08-30, and Anki still runs FSRS-6) and the app keeps a review log ([4.2](#42-the-library-ts-fsrs-542-fsrs-6-now-fsrs-7-later)).
- **When to update CC-CEDICT** to release 2026-10-31 or newer, reviewing the changed entries the app uses ([2.1](#21-dictionary-cc-cedict)).
- **Confidence and expiry** (done): low-confidence phrases, tips and facts carry a "less certain" chip and medium ones a note under their sources; dated travel facts carry `valid_until` and show "out of date" after it ([parts.js](../app/src/views/parts.js), [facts.js](../app/src/views/facts.js); [3.3](#33-travel-facts-that-change-checked-2-november-2026), [3.10](#310-what-is-hidden-and-what-is-left-for-later)).
- **The kinship data model**: `side` is empty for 表哥, 表姐, 表弟 and 表妹 (they can be on either side) and `gender` for 爱人; the schema needs a value such as "either" ([3.4](#34-family)).

## What is UNVERIFIED

[UNVERIFIED.md](UNVERIFIED.md) is generated by the validator (`node scripts/validate.mjs`) and lists every item in the data that the app does not show, with the reason. On 2 November 2026 it holds **17 items**, all from the speaking scenes ([3](#3-the-three-speaking-situations)): 15 that the independent check could not confirm with a second source, and 2 that it refuted.

| Category in UNVERIFIED.md | Items | Travel | Family | Work |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Phrases | 12, 2 of them refuted | 4, 2 of them refuted | 5 | 3 |
| Etiquette tips ("rule") | 5 | 2 | 1 | 2 |
| **Total** | **17** | **6** | **6** | **5** |

- **Refuted:** 劳驾，去中国银行怎么走？ (northern and rather old-fashioned; the app teaches 请问 instead) and 调料里有放肉吗？ (unnatural; the app teaches 这个菜有没有肉？) ([3.1](#31-where-the-content-comes-from-and-how-it-was-checked), [3.2](#32-travel)).
- **No second source:** 我就在书店门口等你。 and 有，您要几张？ (one HSK paper each); 给您拜个晚年！, 学业进步！, 学习进步！, 快快长大！ and 手气最佳; 顺颂商祺, 敬上 and 我司; and the tips on getting ready before your stop, addressing hotel staff, New Year taboos, the order of handshakes and phone etiquette ([3.2](#32-travel), [3.4](#34-family), [3.5](#35-work)). Some of these wordings still appear inside the explanations of verified tips ([3.10](#310-what-is-hidden-and-what-is-left-for-later)).
- **Not on the list:** no HSK word is waiting for a pinyin decision (all 199 differences are decided, [1.9](#19-pinyin-checked-against-cc-cedict)), and all 31 travel facts are verified ([3.3](#33-travel-facts-that-change-checked-2-november-2026)).

Research findings marked **UNVERIFIED** in the text are not app content, so UNVERIFIED.md does not list them. Their number per research topic, from the verified research files, is below; a verified finding can still contain single claims marked **UNVERIFIED** in the text, such as most of the exam dates in [1.5](#15-the-exam-on-2-november-2026).
